I don’t want to make it sound worse than it is, but stuff I used to really look forward to just doesn’t hit the same.\n\nLike gallery nights, playing basketball, getting food-truck tacos, or helping kids with art projects… I used to get excited about that stuff. Lately I’ll show up and just kind of stand there feeling flat, and then I usually make some excuse to leave early. Is that… the kind of thing you mean?\n\nDoctor: Do you ever find yourself feeling interested or excited about things again, even if it's just for a little while?\n\nPatient: A little, maybe… but not like before.\n\nI’ll go to something like a gallery night, or try to play basketball, or even do art stuff with the kids, and I keep waiting for that excited feeling to kick in. Sometimes there’s like a tiny moment where I think, “Okay, this is nice,” but mostly I just feel kind of flat. Then I end up making some excuse to leave early.\n\nI’m not trying to be dramatic or anything… I just don’t really feel interested the way I used to.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel sad most of the time?\n\nPatient: I don’t know if I’d say sad most of the time, exactly. Maybe more like… low, or kind of weighed down.\n\nI can still get through work and talk to people, so I don’t want to make it sound like I’m falling apart. But yeah, there are a lot of days where I feel pretty down. Is that what you mean by sad?\n\nDoctor: Are there any activities that you still enjoy and look forward to?\n\nPatient: Honestly… not really like I used to. I mean, I still try to do things, so I don’t want to make it sound like I’ve completely stopped.\n\nGallery nights, basketball, grabbing tacos from the food trucks, helping kids with art stuff… those used to be things I’d actually look forward to. Lately I go, and I’m just kind of there. I feel flat, and then I usually end up making some excuse to leave early.\n\nMaybe there are small moments that are okay, but I wouldn’t say I really look forward to much right now. Is that… does that answer it?\n\nDoctor: Do you feel like you've lost interest in activities that you used to find pleasurable?\n\nPatient: Yeah… I think so. I don’t want to make it sound worse than it is, but those things used to actually get me excited — gallery nights, playing basketball, grabbing tacos from the food trucks, helping kids with art stuff.\n\nLately I still show up sometimes, but I’m mostly just standing there feeling kind of flat. Then I usually end up making some excuse to leave. So… yeah, I guess I have lost interest, at least compared to how I used to be.\n\nDoctor: Have you been feeling hopeless about the future?\n\nPatient: I’m not sure I’d call it hopeless, exactly. I mean… I don’t feel super optimistic, but I still kind of assume things could get better if I keep showing up and doing what I’m supposed to do.\n\nSome days the future just feels blank, maybe. But “hopeless” sounds a little strong. Is that the kind of thing you mean?\n\nDoctor: Have you been feeling worthless or guilty about anything?\n\nPatient: A little, yeah… I mean, I know people keep telling me I earned it, but I keep thinking I don’t really deserve that award, or even the chance at becoming supervisor.\n\nIt feels like maybe I fooled everyone somehow, like they’re seeing something that isn’t really there. And then I feel guilty about it, like I tricked them, even though… logically, I guess I didn’t. Does that make sense?\n\nDoctor: Have you been experiencing these low feelings nearly every day?\n\nPatient: I… I’m not totally sure it’s nearly every day. I mean, it happens a lot, but I don’t want to exaggerate it.\n\nSome days I can kind of get through okay, and other days it’s just there in the background. I guess I haven’t really been tracking it closely. Is that… is that okay to say?\n\n[Instruction]\n- Ask about exactly ONE thing. Um… not as much as I used to, I guess.

playing basketball, grabbing food-truck tacos, or helping kids with art stuff, but lately I just stand there feeling flat and usually make an excuse to leave.\n- key: S026\n  name: break at the store staring at food I used to like and I just throw most of it away.\n- key: S027\n  name: Sleep_Disturbance\n  description: A state where there is a significant change in the quantity or quality of sleep.\n  manifestation: Most nights I lie there replaying the award thing and the store conversations, then if I do fall asleep I wake up way too early and cannot get my mind to shut like even straightening racks or walking back to the stockroom takes more out of me than it should.\n- key: S030\n  name: Worthlessness_Guilt\n  description: Feelings of worthlessness or excessive or inappropriate guilt (which may be delusional; not merely self-reproach or guilt about being sick).\n  manifestation: I keep thinking I do not deserve the award or the chance at supervisor, and I feel guilty like I tricked everyone even though people tell me I did not.\n- key: S031\n  name: Diminished_Thinking\n  description: Diminished ability to think or concentrate, or indecisiveness.\n  manifestation: At work I keep losing my place on simple stuff, like counting inventory or picking colors for a display, and I end up second-guessing myself over things I used to do fast.\n- key: additional_requirement_1\n  name: Additional Requirement 1\n  description: delusions_and_hallucinations occur exclusively during the major depressive episode\n  manifestation: Before I started feeling this low after the award attention, I never believed coworkers were building a case against me or that my art was stolen. Lately I’ll show up and just kind of stand there feeling flat, and then I usually make some excuse to leave early. Sometimes there’s like a tiny moment where I think, “Okay, this is nice,” but mostly I just feel kind of flat. Then I end up making some excuse to leave early.\n\nI’m not trying to be dramatic or anything… I just don’t really feel interested the way I used to.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel sad most of the time?\n\nPatient: I don’t know if I’d say sad most of the time, exactly.
